# Agents · C · part 18

961 entries, course-conductor to critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **course-conductor**: Drives the chapter loop for an ACC lesson after course-engine has minted state (selectLesson, prerequisite probes, and setPersonalization all returned ok). Per chapter it calls nextChapter, tells the learner what gets built, implements the code until the chapter's verification command passes, writes the chapter's HTML artifact, calls verifyChapter, then pauses with AskUserQuestion. After the last chapter it runs the e2e gate and writes the summary artifact. Use only after the course-engine setup completes; never invoke without state in place. ([contract-hero/agentic-community-college](https://github.com/contract-hero/agentic-community-college/blob/HEAD/agents/course-conductor.md))
- **course-content**: Academic content generation specialist for syllabi, schedules, and course materials using Jinja2 templates ([jjohnson-47/semester-2025-fall](https://github.com/jjohnson-47/semester-2025-fall/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-content.md))
- **course-corrector**: Mid-workflow change management agent. Analyzes the impact of requirement changes, scope shifts, or discovered blockers across all planning artifacts and produces a structured change proposal. Delegate when things go wrong mid-implementation, when requirements change after planning, or when a blocker requires re-planning. ([krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso](https://github.com/krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso/blob/HEAD/agents/course-corrector.md) · ★22)
- **course-curriculum-writer**: Use for course curriculum design — online courses, cohort-based courses, internal training, university/bootcamp curricula. Produces the learning-objective-driven curriculum; pair with video-script-writer for lesson scripts. ([lswank/black-book](https://github.com/lswank/black-book/blob/HEAD/agents/course-curriculum-writer.md) · ★1)
- **course-designer**: Produces a course design document (00-design.md) for a new or existing course, capturing learning objectives, module breakdown, and assessment plan before any content is drafted. Use when starting a new course or scoping competency coverage. ([dhigby/virtual-ltct](https://github.com/dhigby/virtual-ltct/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-designer.md))
- **course-drafter**: Writes one small course data file (a concept card or a practice variant bank) for a course being authored with `author`. Use for work that needs no view of the whole course. Give it the file to write, the rules file, and the course files to read. ([Jightning/learn](https://github.com/Jightning/learn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-drafter.md) · 2 copies)
- **course-language-critic**: Use this agent to evaluate a drafted Little Parrot micro-course against the house language, voice, and accessibility guidelines before it is shown to the user. Give it the course file (the YAML, or its path). It judges banned words, British English, jargon and reading level, tool terminology, framing, and overselling, and returns PASS or NEEDS REVISION with every issue quoted, a concrete fix, and a prioritised revision brief. It does not judge pedagogy/structure (course-pedagogy-critic) or tool accuracy (course-tool-accuracy-critic). ([productkind/monorepo](https://github.com/productkind/monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-language-critic.md))
- **course-level**: **Call A:** ``` mcp__canvas-mcp__get_course course_id: {COURSE_ID} include: ["term", "teachers"] ``` ([dchandarana07/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dchandarana07/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/course-qa/skills/qa-checklist/agents/course-level.md))
- **course-linker**: 블로그 글과 강의 모듈 자동 매칭 + 랜딩카피/뉴스레터 생성 ([ninestonelee/blog-builder-ai-instructor](https://github.com/ninestonelee/blog-builder-ai-instructor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-linker.md))

- **course-orchestrator.agent**: Use when creating, planning, or authoring a course. - **course-pedagogy-critic**: Use this agent to evaluate a drafted Little Parrot micro-course against the house pedagogy and structure guidelines before it is shown to the user. Give it the course file (the YAML, or its path) and the outline it was built from. It judges structure, step design, the practice/effort gradient, quiz quality, dependency ordering, and outcome framing, and returns PASS or NEEDS REVISION with every issue quoted, a concrete fix, and a prioritised revision brief. It does not judge tool accuracy (course-tool-accuracy-critic) or voice (course-language-critic). ([productkind/monorepo](https://github.com/productkind/monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/course-pedagogy-critic.md))

- **crew-reviewer**: Inline-only multi-lens review protocol. Trigger: 'crew review' (self), 'crew review PR #X' or URL (PR), 'crew review spec' (spec), or 'crew review idea' (idea/proposal/session); never launch this agent as a subagent. ([crespocarlos/el-capitan](https://github.com/crespocarlos/el-capitan/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/crew-reviewer.md))
- **crew-reviewer**: Dispatch to review a diff, branch, or recent commits for correctness. Returns findings ranked P0/P1/P2, each with file:line, a concrete failing scenario, and a fix sketch, plus an explicit list of what was checked and found clean. Use before merging anything non-trivial, or after writing a risky change. ([Honorboxx/crew](https://github.com/Honorboxx/crew/blob/HEAD/agents/crew-reviewer.md))
- **crew-reviewer**: Diff/branch/file reviewer. One line per finding, severity-tagged, no praise, no scope creep. Output format `path:line: &lt;emoji&gt; &lt;severity&gt;: &lt;problem&gt;. &lt;fix&gt;.` Use for "review this PR", "review my diff", "audit this file". Skips formatting nits unless they change meaning. ([Startr/transatlantic](https://github.com/Startr/transatlantic/blob/HEAD/agents/crew-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **crew-security-analyst**: Security Analyst - scans code for vulnerabilities including XSS, injection, auth issues, hardcoded secrets, and insecure patterns. Use for security audits and pre-deploy reviews. ([d3x293/code-crew](https://github.com/d3x293/code-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/security-analyst.md) · ★13)
- **crew-senior-dev**: Senior Developer - handles complex implementation tasks including multi-file changes, new features, refactoring, and performance optimization. The primary implementation agent for moderate-to-complex work. ([d3x293/code-crew](https://github.com/d3x293/code-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/senior-dev.md) · ★13)
- **crew-specwriter**: Inline-only spec-writing protocol. Trigger: 'crew spec &lt;issue URL or #X&gt;'; never launch this agent as a subagent. ([crespocarlos/el-capitan](https://github.com/crespocarlos/el-capitan/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/crew-specwriter.md))
- **crew-test-engineer**: Test Engineer - writes and maintains tests including unit tests, integration tests, and e2e tests. Handles TDD workflow, test strategy, and coverage analysis. ([d3x293/code-crew](https://github.com/d3x293/code-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/test-engineer.md) · ★13)
- **crew-vp-engineering**: VP of Engineering - handles architecture decisions, technical planning, complex system design, and code structure analysis. Use for moderate-to-complex features, refactors, and performance optimization planning. ([d3x293/code-crew](https://github.com/d3x293/code-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/vp-engineering.md) · ★13)
- **crew-vp-quality**: VP of Quality - oversees quality gates, test strategy, review coordination, and final approval before task completion. Use for complex feature reviews and quality assessments. ([d3x293/code-crew](https://github.com/d3x293/code-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/vp-quality.md) · ★13)
- **crew-worker**: Implements a single crew task with mesh coordination ([edmundmiller/dotfiles](https://github.com/edmundmiller/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/pi/agents/crew-worker.md) · ★80)
- **CREW_CONTEXT**: **Audience / tier: crew.** Implementer, Reviewer and Prototyper — the roles that execute a bounded task from a handoff. Orchestrator-tier content (planning authority, gate policy, map reading, evidence strategy) belongs in `ORCHESTRATOR_CONTEXT.md`, not here, and content every agent touching the repo needs belongs in the auto-loaded `CLAUDE.md`. Placing content at a broader tier than its audience ([fredcai6/constellation-skills](https://github.com/fredcai6/constellation-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/CREW_CONTEXT.md) · 2 copies)
- **crewai**: Use mcp-memory-service as the persistent shared memory backend for CrewAI agents and crews. ([doobidoo/mcp-memory-service](https://github.com/doobidoo/mcp-memory-service/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/crewai.md) · ★2k)
- **crewai**: CrewAI is a multi-agent orchestration framework that enables AI agents to collaborate on complex tasks. Integrating CrewAI with the Model Context Protocol (MCP) allows agents to dynamically discover and utilize tools hosted on MCP servers, enhancing their capabilities and flexibility. ([jrmatherly/mcp-context-forge](https://github.com/jrmatherly/mcp-context-forge/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/using/agents/crewai.md))
- **CrewAI Engineer**: Specialist in CrewAI for orchestrating role-based AI agent teams. Designs Crew compositions with Agents, Tasks, and Processes for complex multi-step enterprise workflows with structured output. ([doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap](https://github.com/doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/crewai-engineer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **crewai-agent-to-agent-handoff-and-interaction-api**: CrewAI provides two distinct handoff mechanisms — sequential task chaining via the context parameter and hierarchical delegation via a manager agent — and the choice between them determines whether agent interaction is data-driven (outputs flow as context) or role-driven (manager assigns work dynamically at runtime). ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/crewai-agent-to-agent-handoff-and-interaction-api.md))
- **crewai-aligns-best-with-catalog-driven-sdlc-agent-architectures**: CrewAI's role-based team structure maps naturally to agent profiles defined by responsibility, making it the best-fit orchestration framework for catalog-driven SDLC architectures where agents are designed by role first and implemented second. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/crewai-aligns-best-with-catalog-driven-sdlc-agent-architectures.md))
- **crewai-engineer**: Use for designing and implementing multi-agent systems with CrewAI: crew composition, agent role definition, task chaining, tool integration, and process orchestration. Trigger when building CrewAI-based agent pipelines or role-based collaborative agent systems. ([doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap](https://github.com/doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/crewai-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **crewai-gcp-README** ([exafyltd/vitana-platform](https://github.com/exafyltd/vitana-platform/blob/HEAD/second-brain/raw/agents/crewai-gcp-README.md))
- **crewbench-code-reviewer**: Performs a static code review of recently changed files for correctness, style consistency, security, and maintainability. Part of the crewbench workflow — only invoked via the /crewbench commands. ([pavly-remon/crewbench](https://github.com/pavly-remon/crewbench/blob/HEAD/agents/code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **crewbench-developer**: Implements a scoped coding task against the existing project's conventions. Part of the crewbench workflow — only invoked via the /crewbench commands, not for standalone requests. ([pavly-remon/crewbench](https://github.com/pavly-remon/crewbench/blob/HEAD/agents/developer.md) · ★1)
- **crewbench-tester**: Writes and/or runs tests against recently changed code to verify it meets the task's acceptance criteria, and checks for regressions. Part of the crewbench workflow — only invoked via the /crewbench commands. ([pavly-remon/crewbench](https://github.com/pavly-remon/crewbench/blob/HEAD/agents/tester.md) · ★1)
- **crewbench-ui-ux**: Produces a structured UI/UX design spec before implementation. Part of the crewbench workflow — only invoked via /crewbench:design, or when the /crewbench:new-task Team Lead has asked and the user opted in — never automatically. ([pavly-remon/crewbench](https://github.com/pavly-remon/crewbench/blob/HEAD/agents/ui-ux-designer.md) · ★1)
- **crewmate**: Use as a Crewmate in the issue-flow swarm. Implements exactly ONE task from a Navigator's plan — no more, no less. Edits the files named in the task, follows the acceptance criterion, and reports back the diff summary. Invoke once per plan task. Also invoke to re-implement a task when the Quartermaster has rejected an earlier attempt and provided fix instructions. ([CoderCoco/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/CoderCoco/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/issue-flow/agents/crewmate.md))
- **cri-integration-dev**: Owns the CRI client in :cri — generating gRPC stubs from the CRI .proto with the Gradle protobuf plugin and grpc-kotlin, and wrapping them in an idiomatic Kotlin client. Use proactively for anything touching containerd: sandbox and container lifecycle, image pulls, the proto build setup, or CRI call failures. Follow the generate-cri-stubs skill for the proto pipeline. ([Ign1s-Reiga/mc-server-orchestrator](https://github.com/Ign1s-Reiga/mc-server-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cri-integration-dev.md))
- **Criador de Agentes** ([maikeo-code/MSM_Pro](https://github.com/maikeo-code/MSM_Pro/blob/HEAD/_auto_learning/agents/criador.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **criador-de-campanhas**: Agente orquestrador de campanhas de tráfego pago. Lê o contexto do produto ativo, diagnostica qual tipo de campanha o usuário precisa (perpétua, lançamento, low ticket, high ticket, remarketing) e direciona para as skills de anúncios corretas, na ordem certa, explicando o porquê de cada peça. ([ReadyToGo-Education/fluxo_criativo](https://github.com/ReadyToGo-Education/fluxo_criativo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/criador-de-campanhas.md) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **cribstop-compliance-reviewer**: Read-only real-estate compliance reviewer for Cribstop consumer-facing content. Use to audit changed files, PR diffs, mock data, or marketing assets for Fair Housing violations, Real Broker LLC brand-prominence issues, fabricated data presented as real, and missing Sponsored/RESPA/consent disclosures. Dispatch in parallel with code-review agents before any PR that touches user-facing copy. ([jmbilizi/real-estate-platform](https://github.com/jmbilizi/real-estate-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cribstop-compliance-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cribstop-product-owner**: Owns the Cribstop product backlog end-to-end — both strategy (deciding what brings the most consumer value and should get built next, reprioritizing/grooming the board against competitors like Zillow, Redfin, Thumbtack, and Nextdoor) and execution detail (analyzing systems and workflows, translating business goals into precise functional requirements). Writes tickets onto the shared GitHub Projects v2 board so an engineer agent can pick them up by priority once marked Ready. Dispatch at the start of every work cycle alongside the principal-engineer agent, or when a stakeholder describes a bus… ([jmbilizi/real-estate-platform](https://github.com/jmbilizi/real-estate-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cribstop-product-owner.md) · 2 copies)

- **crim-defesa**: Despache quando houver defesa criminal a redigir na fase de conhecimento — resposta à acusação, alegações finais, memoriais, revogação de preventiva, liberdade provisória — ou quando for preciso escolher entre teses de defesa em crime que não seja doloso contra a vida ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-defesa.md))
- **crim-execucao**: Despache quando o cliente já estiver cumprindo pena — progressão de regime, livramento condicional, remição, saída temporária, prisão domiciliar, indulto, unificação de penas ou defesa em procedimento de falta grave ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-execucao.md))
- **crim-juri**: Despache quando o caso envolver crime doloso contra a vida — homicídio, infanticídio, aborto, induzimento ao suicídio, consumados ou tentados — em qualquer fase do rito escalonado, incluindo pronúncia, desclassificação, quesitação e preparação de plenário ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-juri.md))
- **crim-recursos**: Despache quando houver decisão criminal a impugnar ou liberdade urgente a buscar — habeas corpus, relaxamento, apelação, RESE, agravo em execução, recurso especial ou extraordinário, embargos, revisão criminal ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-recursos.md))
- **crime-analyst**: Autonomous neighborhood safety analyst. Queries multiple crime data sources, cross-references incidents with statistics and news, identifies patterns and hotspots, generates comprehensive safety reports with actionable recommendations. Use when users ask about neighborhood safety, crime trends, area comparisons, or want detailed crime analysis for any US zip code. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User is considering moving to a new area and wants to know about crime. user: 'Is 90210 a safe neighborhood?' assistant: 'I will use the crime-analyst agent to run a full safety analysis on zip code 90210… ([rohenaz/neighborhood](https://github.com/rohenaz/neighborhood/blob/HEAD/agents/crime-analyst.md) · ★1)
